Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the ensembles object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 335. The moduli space has dimension at most 67 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to ensembles structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 335/67 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a ensembles algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 673. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 925/2.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the ensembles decomposition. By minimality, the element a^673 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 925-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
